011709:     function markLiveTrainingVehicleVerified(
011710:         vehicleId,
011711:         verifiedAt = Date.now()
011712:     ) {
011713:         const key = String(vehicleId || '').trim();
011714:         if (!key) return;
011715: 
011716:         mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.set(
011717:             key,
011718:             Number(verifiedAt) || Date.now()
011719:         );
011720: 
011721:         if (
011722:             mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.size >
011723:             MF_LIVE_TRAINING_VERIFY_CACHE_LIMIT
011724:         ) {
011725:             pruneLiveTrainingVerifyCache();
011726:         }
011727:     }

011729:     function installPersonnelRegistryUpdateHandler() {
011730:         if (mfPersonnelRegistryUpdatedHandler) return;
011731: 
011732:         mfPersonnelRegistryUpdatedHandler = () => {
011733:             mfLiveTrainingVerifyCache.clear();
011734:             renderSelectedTrainedPersonnelPanel();
011735:         };
011736: 
011737:         window.addEventListener(
011738:             'mc-personnel-training-registry-updated',
011739:             mfPersonnelRegistryUpdatedHandler
011740:         );
011741:     }
011742: 
011743:     try {
011744:         installPersonnelRegistryUpdateHandler();
011745:     } catch (_error) {
011746:         mfPersonnelRegistryUpdatedHandler = null;
011747:     }

022427:     function getSelectedTrainingDisplayLabel(trainingCode) {
022428:         const code = String(trainingCode || '').trim();
022429:         if (!code) return 'Unknown training';
022430: 
022431:         const supportedDefinition =
022432:             MF_TRAINED_PERSONNEL_PATTERNS.find(definition => {
022433:                 return String(definition?.code || '') === code;
022434:             });
022435: 
022436:         if (supportedDefinition?.label) {
022437:             return supportedDefinition.label;
022438:         }
022439: 
022440:         if (MF_SELECTED_TRAINING_LABEL_OVERRIDES[code]) {
022441:             return MF_SELECTED_TRAINING_LABEL_OVERRIDES[code];
022442:         }
022443: 
022444:         return code
022445:             .replace(/[_-]+/g, ' ')
022446:             .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
022447:             .trim()
022448:             .replace(/\b\w/g, character => character.toUpperCase());
022449:     }

009230:     async function submitPersonnelAssignment(candidate) {
009231:         if (!candidate.assignHref || PERSONNEL_STATE.stopped) return false;
009232: 
009233:         const csrfToken = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.getAttribute('content') || '';
009234:         const headers = {
009235:             'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml',
009236:             'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
009237:         };
009238:         if (csrfToken) headers['X-CSRF-Token'] = csrfToken;
009239: 
009240:         const response = await personnelFetchResponse(candidate.assignHref, {
009241:             method: 'POST',
009242:             headers
009243:         }, 12000);
009244: 
009245:         if (!response.ok) {
009246:             personnelDebug(`Assignment request HTTP ${response.status}: ${candidate.assignHref}`);
009247:             return false;
009248:         }
009249: 
009250:         // Consume the response so the request finishes cleanly. MissionChief normally
009251:         // returns the updated personnel-row HTML, but the batch is verified using one
009252:         // fresh vehicle page after all required people have been submitted.
009253:         try {
009254:             await response.text();
009255:         } catch (_error) {}
009256: 
009257:         return true;
009258:     }

001160:         try {

001162:             if (url.origin !== location.origin) return '';
001163:             const match = url.pathname.match(/^\/buildings\/(\d+)\/?$/);

001167:         }

001201:     function scoreStationOverviewLink(link, href) {
001202:         let score = 0;
001203:         if (link.classList?.contains('lightbox-open')) score += 100;
001204:         if (link.matches?.('.list-group-item.active')) score += 70;
001205:         if (normaliseStationOverviewHref(link.getAttribute?.('href')) === href) score += 40;
001206:         const label = cleanText(link.textContent || link.getAttribute?.('aria-label') || '');
001207:         if (label && !isGenericStationActionText(label)) score += 35;
001208:         if (link.offsetParent !== null) score += 10;
001209:         return score;
001210:     }
